# Country profiles — Asia and Pacific

IMF World Economic Outlook, IMF Financial Soundness Indicators and credit-to-GDP gaps (BIS, or IMF Monetary and Financial Statistics for Kuwait, UAE, Qatar and Oman) for 37 economies in Asia and Pacific. Each country has its own section below. Built 2026-09-28 from github.com/Aknarik/country-data.

## Australia — credit-to-GDP ratio, Hodrick-Prescott trend and credit gap

Australia: One-sided HP filter, lambda = 400,000; trend from 10 years after the series start. Gap = ratio minus trend, percentage points of GDP. Basel III countercyclical buffer guide: gap above 2 pp may signal a buffer build-up; above 10 pp the maximum buffer. Gap mini chart (2023-Q2 to 2026-Q1): ▁▂▂▃▄▄▆▆▇▇██ (min -26.3, max -9.7).

## Hong Kong SAR — credit-to-GDP ratio, Hodrick-Prescott trend and credit gap

Hong Kong SAR: One-sided HP filter, lambda = 400,000; trend from 10 years after the series start. Gap = ratio minus trend, percentage points of GDP. ## India — credit-to-GDP ratio, Hodrick-Prescott trend and credit gap

India: One-sided HP filter, lambda = 400,000; trend from 10 years after the series start. Gap = ratio minus trend, percentage points of GDP. Basel III countercyclical buffer guide: gap above 2 pp may signal a buffer build-up; above 10 pp the maximum buffer. Gap mini chart (2023-Q2 to 2026-Q1): ▁▂▂▃▃▃▄▄▅▅▇█ (min -12.3, max 4.3).

## Korea — credit-to-GDP ratio, Hodrick-Prescott trend and credit gap

Korea: One-sided HP filter, lambda = 400,000; trend from 10 years after the series start. Gap = ratio minus trend, percentage points of GDP. ## Malaysia — credit-to-GDP ratio, Hodrick-Prescott trend and credit gap

Malaysia: One-sided HP filter, lambda = 400,000; trend from 10 years after the series start. Gap = ratio minus trend, percentage points of GDP. Basel III countercyclical buffer guide: gap above 2 pp may signal a buffer build-up; above 10 pp the maximum buffer. Gap mini chart (2023-Q2 to 2026-Q1): ▁▃▅▅▄▂▄▅▆▇▇█ (min -9.2, max -2.8).

## New Zealand — credit-to-GDP ratio, Hodrick-Prescott trend and credit gap

New Zealand: One-sided HP filter, lambda = 400,000; trend from 10 years after the series start. Gap = ratio minus trend, percentage points of GDP. ## Singapore — credit-to-GDP ratio, Hodrick-Prescott trend and credit gap

Singapore: One-sided HP filter, lambda = 400,000; trend from 10 years after the series start. Gap = ratio minus trend, percentage points of GDP. Basel III countercyclical buffer guide: gap above 2 pp may signal a buffer build-up; above 10 pp the maximum buffer. Gap mini chart (2023-Q2 to 2026-Q1): ▃▃▄▃▃▁▂▃▄▅█▇ (min -37.9, max -19.8).

## Thailand — credit-to-GDP ratio, Hodrick-Prescott trend and credit gap

Thailand: One-sided HP filter, lambda = 400,000; trend from 10 years after the series start. Gap = ratio minus trend, percentage points of GDP. Basel III countercyclical buffer guide: gap above 2 pp may signal a buffer build-up; above 10 pp the maximum buffer. Gap mini chart (2023-Q2 to 2026-Q1): ███▇▆▅▅▄▃▄▁▁ (min -15.8, max 8.3).
